 vmo:{min:80,max:700,lab:'VMO',u:'KIAS',d:340},mmo:{min:0.20,max:1.00,lab:'MMO',u:'Mach',d:0.81},
 stepAlt:{min:0,max:65000,lab:'Stepped altitude',u:'ft',d:null},stepMach:{min:0.20,max:1.00,lab:'Stepped MMO',u:'Mach',d:null},
 alt:{min:-500,max:65000,lab:'Pressure altitude',u:'ft',d:35000},oat:{min:-110,max:60,lab:'OAT',u:'deg C',d:null},
 ice:{min:0,max:60,lab:'Ice accretion',u:'%',d:0},mach:{min:0.05,max:1.30,lab:'Mach',u:'',d:0.76},
 mStall:{min:0,max:60,lab:'Stall margin',u:'kt',d:10},mVmo:{min:0,max:60,lab:'Ceiling margin',u:'kt',d:10}};
function resolve(){
 var iss=[],val={},used=[];
 for(var k in RU){
  var r=RU[k],v=V(k);
  if(v===null||!isFinite(v)){
   if(r.d===null){val[k]=null;continue;}
   val[k]=r.def;used.push(r.lab);iss.push(r.lab+' blank - using default '+r.def+' '+r.u);
  }else if(v<r.min){val[k]=r.min;iss.push(r.lab+' below minimum, clamped');}
  else if(v>r.max){val[k]=r.max;iss.push(r.lab+' above maximum, clamped');}
  else val[k]=v;
 }
 if((val.stepAlt===null)!==(val.stepMach===null)){iss.push('Enter BOTH stepped altitude and stepped MMO, or neither');val.stepAlt=null;val.stepMach=null;}
 if(val.weight>val.wRef*1.02)iss.push('Above reference weight - sqrt(W) scaling is first-order');
 if(val.alt>50000)iss.push('Above FL500 - beyond any business-jet service ceiling');
 if(val.alt>36089)iss.push('Above the tropopause (36,089 ft): the ISA is ISOTHERMAL, so a = 497.9 kt at EVERY level and the stall Mach rises from DENSITY ALONE');
 if(val.ice>25)iss.push('Ice accretion above the severe range - verify against the recovered airframe');
 if(val.oat!==null){var d=isaAt(val.alt).Tc-val.oat;if(Math.abs(d)>25)iss.push('Large ISA deviation '+F(d,1)+' deg C');}
 if(val.oat!==null&&val.oat<-10&&val.ice>0)iss.push('ICING-TYPE CHECK: at '+F(val.oat,0)+' deg C, supercooled liquid water (and therefore CLEAR ice) should be essentially absent - expect ice-crystal or rime. Verify the temperature, the sounding and the recovered airframe.');
 if(val.oat!==null&&val.oat>=-2&&val.oat<=6&&val.ice>0)iss.push('ICING-TYPE CHECK: this is the SUPERCOOLED-LIQUID-WATER MAXIMUM (0 to +5 deg C). Severe icing is possible and the de/anti-ice may not be able to keep up.');
 if(val.mach>1.0)iss.push('Above Mach 1.0');
 return {val:val,iss:iss,used:used};
}
var PRE={lr60:{vsRef:185,wRef:27500,vmo:340,mmo:0.81,stepAlt:37000,stepMach:0.78,
 t:'Learjet 60 TEACHING CASE - ILLUSTRATIVE (commentary-chart values, NOT certified data). Replace with your own AFM.'},
 custom:{t:'Custom - enter Vs at reference weight, reference weight, VMO and MMO from your AFM, plus the stepped limit if you have one.'}};
function fillPreset(force){
 var p=PRE[$('preset').value];
 ['vsRef','wRef','vmo','mmo','stepAlt','stepMach'].forEach(function(k){var e=$(k);if(!e)return;if(p[k]!==undefined){if(force||!e.value)e.value=p[k];}});
 T('pHint',p.t);
 H('vsHint',$('preset').value==='lr60'?'Chart-implied stall line back-calculated at about 185 KIAS. ILLUSTRATIVE - not an AFM figure.':'From your AFM. Scaled as sqrt(weight / reference weight).');
}
function compute(v){
 var A=atmos(v.alt,v.oat);
 var vsW=v.vsRef*Math.sqrt(v.weight/v.wRef)+v.cfgInc,vsIce=vsW*(1+v.ice/100);
 var as=A.aSig,ok=(as>100&&as<700);
 var Mst=vsIce/as,MstC=vsW/as,ias=v.mach*as,tas=v.mach*A.aKt;
 var sm=($('stepMode')&&$('stepMode').value==='gt')?'gt':'ge';
 var step=(v.stepAlt!==null&&v.stepMach!==null&&(sm==='gt'?v.alt>v.stepAlt:v.alt>=v.stepAlt));
 var mmoEff=step?v.stepMach:v.mmo,mVmo=v.vmo/as;
 var cd=[[mmoEff,'MMO'+(step?' (stepped)':'')],[mVmo,'VMO '+v.vmo+' KIAS']].sort(function(a,b){return a[0]-b[0];});
 var ceil=cd[0][0],src=cd[0][1],iasC=ceil*as,tasC=ceil*A.aKt;
 var floor=(vsIce+v.mStall)/as,cpol=ceil-(v.mVmo)/as,closed=(floor>=cpol);
 var Vv=v.mach*A.aMs,q=0.5*A.rho*Vv*Vv,dens=A.rho/REF.rho,spd=Math.pow(Vv/REF.V,2);
 var qC=0.5*A.rho*Math.pow(ceil*A.aMs,2);
 var sKt=ias-vsIce,cKt=iasC-ias;
 var over=v.mach>ceil+1e-9,under=v.mach<Mst-1e-9,ufl=v.mach<floor-1e-9,opol=v.mach>cpol+1e-9;
 var verd='GO',cls='go',why=[],act=[],adv=[];
 if(!ok){verd='UNIT ERROR SUSPECTED';cls='no';why.push('a*sqrt(sigma) = '+F(as,1)+' kt is outside the plausible range 100-700 kt. A unit error is likely.');act.push('Do not use these figures.');}
 else if(closed){verd='NO-GO - BAND CLOSED';cls='no';why.push('No speed is simultaneously '+F(v.mStall,0)+' kt above the icing stall (M'+F(floor,3)+') and '+F(v.mVmo,0)+' kt below the ceiling (M'+F(cpol,3)+').');act.push('DESCEND. Change altitude, not speed.');}
 else if(over){verd='NO-GO - OVERSPEED';cls='no';why.push('Mach '+F(v.mach,3)+' exceeds the '+src+' ceiling of M'+F(ceil,3)+'.');act.push('Reduce speed and level the wings. Above the AFM limit there is NO demonstrated structural or flutter margin.');}
 else if(under){verd='NO-GO - BELOW STALL';cls='no';why.push('Mach '+F(v.mach,3)+' is below the icing-adjusted stall Mach M'+F(Mst,3)+' ('+F(vsIce,1)+' KIAS).');act.push('Add energy now: pitch up, increase thrust. Expect no buffet warning in ice and a possible asymmetric break.');}
 else if(ufl){verd='NO-GO - STALL MARGIN INSUFFICIENT';cls='no';why.push('Only '+F(sKt,1)+' kt above the icing stall; policy requires '+F(v.mStall,0)+' kt.');act.push('Increase to at least M'+F(floor,3)+' if the ceiling allows, or descend out of the icing.');}
 else if(opol){verd='CAUTION - OVERSPEED MARGIN INSUFFICIENT';cls='caution';why.push('Below the hard ceiling but only '+F(cKt,1)+' kt below it; policy requires '+F(v.mVmo,0)+' kt.');act.push('Reduce Mach toward M'+F(cpol,3)+', or descend to open the band.');}
 else{why.push('Mach '+F(v.mach,3)+' is inside the policy window with '+F(sKt,1)+' kt above the icing stall and '+F(cKt,1)+' kt below the ceiling.');}
 if(step)adv.push({c:'info',t:'<b>Stepped limit applies:</b> '+src+'. The more restrictive of the two limits binds.'});
 if(over||opol)adv.push({c:'bad',t:'At or near the AFM speed limit. Beyond MMO there is no demonstrated margin - control-surface flutter and structural overstress become credible.'});
 if(v.ice>0)adv.push({c:'',t:'<b>Icing:</b> stall '+F(vsW,1)+' to '+F(vsIce,1)+' KIAS (+'+F(v.ice,0)+' percent). Ice lowers the stall angle of attack (buffet may never appear), degrades ailerons, cuts elevator power, and adds weight.'});
 if(v.ice>0)adv.push({c:'info',t:'<b>THE COMPUTER IS GOING TO BE WRONG.</b> FMS and autothrust climb performance assumes a CLEAN wing. In icing your actual climb is worse than displayed, and the cleared level may be physically unreachable. Override the automation when the aircraft behaves differently from the machine model.'});
 if(closed&&ok)adv.push({c:'bad',t:'<b>The usable band is CLOSED</b> at this altitude, weight, configuration and ice state. Altitude is the only variable that helps.'});
 if(v.mach>0.80)adv.push({c:'',t:'Above M0.80: Mach tuck reduces elevator effectiveness. Do not pull at high Mach.'});
 if(v.alt>36089)adv.push({c:'info',t:'Above the tropopause the ISA is isothermal: a = 497.9 kt at every level. The stall Mach rises here from DENSITY ALONE - sound speed no longer contributes.'});
 if(A.dev!==0)adv.push({c:'info',t:'OAT is '+F(Math.abs(A.dev),1)+' deg C '+((A.dev>0)?'colder':'warmer')+' than ISA here. Estimated geometric altitude about '+F(A.geom,0)+' ft.'});
 var zeroIce=(vsW>0)?(ias/vsW-1)*100:0;
 var closedIce=(vsW>0)?((iasC-v.mVmo)/vsW*100-100):0;
 return {A:A,vsW:vsW,vsIce:vsIce,Mst:Mst,MstC:MstC,ias:ias,tas:tas,ceil:ceil,src:src,iasC:iasC,tasC:tasC,
  floor:floor,cpol:cpol,closed:closed,rawK:iasC-vsIce,winK:(cpol-floor)*as,q:q,qC:qC,dens:dens,spd:spd,qR:q/REF.q,
  sKt:sKt,cKt:cKt,step:step,mmoEff:mmoEff,mVmo:mVmo,as:as,ok:ok,verd:verd,cls:cls,why:why,act:act,adv:adv,
  zeroIce:zeroIce,closedIce:closedIce};
}
function iceTable(r,v){
 var rows=[],pcts=[0,5,10,12.5,15,20],i;
 for(i=0;i<pcts.length;i++){
  var p=pcts[i],vic=r.vsW*(1+p/100),mst=vic/r.as,mk=r.ias-vic;
  var fl=(vic+v.mStall)/r.as,cp=r.ceil-v.mVmo/r.as,wid=(cp-fl)*r.as;
  var st=(mk<=0)?'BELOW THE STALL':((fl>=cp)?'BAND CLOSED':((mk<v.mStall)?'MARGIN BELOW POLICY':'OK'));
  var cls=(mk<=0)?'zero':((fl>=cp)?'zero':((mk<v.mStall)?'bad2':''));
  rows.push('<tr class="'+cls+'"><td>'+p+'%</td><td>'+F(vic,1)+'</td><td>M'+F(mst,3)+'</td><td>'+F(mk,1)+'</td><td>'+F(wid,2)+'</td><td>'+st+'</td></tr>');
 }
 fill('iceTbl',rows.join(''));
}
function renderBand(){
 try{
 var rs=resolve(),v=rs.val,r=compute(v),mach=v.mach;
 var ve=$('verdict');ve.className='v '+r.cls;ve.innerHTML=r.verd+'<small>'+r.why.join(' ')+'</small>';
 var rows=[];
 function row(l,M,ta,ia){rows.push('<tr'+(l.indexOf('YOUR MACH')>=0?' style="font-weight:700"':'')+'><td>'+l+'</td><td>'+F(M,3)+'</td><td>'+F(ta,1)+'</td><td>'+F(ia,1)+'</td><td>'+(ia===null?'--':F(ia-r.vsIce,1))+'</td><td>'+F(M-mach,3)+'</td></tr>');}
 row('Stall - clean',r.MstC,r.MstC*r.A.aKt,r.vsW);
 row('Stall - +'+v.ice+'% ice',r.Mst,r.Mst*r.A.aKt,r.vsIce);
 row('Policy floor (stall +'+v.mStall+' kt)',r.floor,r.floor*r.A.aKt,r.vsIce+v.mStall);
 row('YOUR MACH',mach,r.tas,r.ias);
 row('Policy ceiling (limit -'+v.mVmo+' kt)',r.cpol,r.cpol*r.A.aKt,r.iasC-v.mVmo);
 row('Ceiling - '+r.src,r.ceil,r.tasC,r.iasC);
 fill('bt',rows.join(''));
 T('bNote','Raw band (hard boundaries, no margin) = '+F(r.rawK,1)+' kt ('+F(r.ceil-r.Mst,3)+' Mach). Policy window = '+F(r.winK,2)+' kt (M'+F(r.floor,3)+' to M'+F(r.cpol,3)+'). '+(r.closed?'POLICY WINDOW CLOSED.':'Outside it means less than '+v.mStall+' kt of stall margin or less than '+v.mVmo+' kt of ceiling margin.'));
 var lo=Math.min(0.35,r.floor-0.06),hi=Math.max(0.90,r.ceil+0.06);
 lo=Math.floor(lo*20)/20;hi=Math.ceil(hi*20)/20;
 function pc(m){return Math.max(0,Math.min(100,(m-lo)/(hi-lo)*100));}
 var zr=$('zRaw'),zs=$('zSafe'),ms=$('mStallMk'),mc=$('mCeil'),mu=$('mCur');
 if(zr){zr.style.left=pc(r.Mst)+'%';zr.style.width=(pc(r.ceil)-pc(r.Mst))+'%';}
 if(zs){if(r.closed){zs.style.left='0%';zs.style.width='0%';}else{zs.style.left=pc(r.floor)+'%';zs.style.width=Math.max(0,pc(r.cpol)-pc(r.floor))+'%';}}
 if(ms)ms.style.left=pc(r.Mst)+'%';if(mc)mc.style.left=pc(r.ceil)+'%';if(mu)mu.style.left=pc(mach)+'%';
 var ls=$('lStall'),lc2=$('lCeil'),lu=$('lCur');
 if(ls){ls.style.left=Math.min(pc(r.Mst)+4,66)+'%';ls.textContent='stall M'+F(r.Mst,3);}
 if(lc2){lc2.style.left=Math.max(pc(r.ceil)-36,2)+'%';lc2.textContent='ceiling M'+F(r.ceil,3);}
 if(lu){lu.style.left=Math.min(pc(mach)+2,84)+'%';lu.textContent='YOU M'+F(mach,3);}
 var sc='';for(var mm=lo;mm<=hi+1e-9;mm+=0.1)sc+='<span style="left:'+pc(mm)+'%">M'+mm.toFixed(2)+'</span>';
 H('scale',sc);
 iceTable(r,v);
 var cs=r.sKt<0?'bad':(r.sKt<v.mStall?'warn':'good'),cc=r.cKt<0?'bad':(r.cKt<v.mVmo?'warn':'good');
 H('kvs',
  Ktile('Stall margin',F(mach-r.Mst,3),F(r.sKt,1)+' kt above the icing stall',cs)+
  Ktile('Overspeed margin',F(r.ceil-mach,3),F(r.cKt,1)+' kt below the ceiling',cc)+
  Ktile('q at your Mach',F(r.q/PSF,0)+' psf',F(r.q,0)+' Pa',r.qR>1.2?'warn':'info')+
  Ktile('q vs cruise',F(r.qR,2)+'x',F(r.dens,2)+' density x '+F(r.spd,2)+' speed',r.qR>1.5?'warn':'')+
  Ktile('q vs limit q',F(r.q/r.qC,3)+'x','(M/MMO)^2',r.q/r.qC>1?'bad':'good')+
  Ktile('Your IAS',F(r.ias,1)+' K','TAS '+F(r.tas,1)+' kt','')+
  Ktile('Icing stall',F(r.vsIce,1)+' K','M'+F(r.Mst,3)+' clean '+F(r.vsW,1)+' K','info')+
  Ktile('Margin hits zero at',F(r.zeroIce,1)+'%','ice accretion. Policy window closed at '+F(r.closedIce,1)+'%','warn'));
 var qr=[];
 function qrow(l,M){var Vv=M*r.A.aMs,q2=0.5*r.A.rho*Vv*Vv;qr.push('<tr><td>'+l+'</td><td>'+F(M,3)+'</td><td>'+F(M*r.A.aKt,1)+'</td><td>'+F(q2,0)+'</td><td>'+F(q2/PSF,0)+'</td><td>'+F(q2/REF.q,2)+'x</td></tr>');}
 qrow('Your Mach',mach);qrow('Policy ceiling',r.cpol);qrow('Hard ceiling ('+r.src+')',r.ceil);qrow('Stall +'+v.ice+'% ice',r.Mst);
 fill('qt',qr.join(''));
 T('qNote','Reference cruise = 35,000 ft, M0.76, ISA (q = '+F(REF.q/PSF,0)+' psf). In a descent the air thickens AND true airspeed rises: Mach can fall while q climbs. Track q, not Mach.');
 var A=r.A;
 H('atm',
  Ktile('Pressure altitude',N(v.alt)+' ft','ISA here '+F(A.Tc,1)+' deg C','')+
  Ktile('OAT',v.oat===null?'ISA':F(v.oat,0)+' deg C','ISA deviation '+F(A.dev,1)+' deg C','info')+
  Ktile('Est. geometric alt',A.geom?F(A.geom,0)+' ft':'--','from OAT + pressure alt','info')+
  Ktile('Sound speed a',F(A.aKt,1)+' kt',F(A.aMs,1)+' m/s = '+F(A.aMph,1)+' mph','')+
  Ktile('Density sigma',F(A.sig,4),F(A.rho,4)+' kg/m3','')+
  Ktile('a * sqrt(sigma)',F(A.as,1)+' kt','IAS per unit Mach',''));
 T('work',
'ATMOSPHERE\n'+
'  h_m   = '+N(v.alt)+' ft x 0.3048                    = '+F(A.I.hm,1)+' m\n'+
'  T_ISA = 288.15 - 0.0065*h_m                     = '+F(A.Tc,2)+' deg C  ('+F(A.I.T,2)+' K)\n'+
'  p     = 101325*(1 - 0.0065*h_m/288.15)^5.25588   = '+F(A.p,0)+' Pa\n'+
'  T     = '+(v.oat===null?'T_ISA (OAT blank)':(F(v.oat,1)+' deg C + 273.15'))+'               = '+F(A.T,2)+' K\n'+
'  rho   = p/(R*T) = '+F(A.p,0)+'/(287.053*'+F(A.T,2)+')        = '+F(A.rho,5)+' kg/m3\n'+
'  sigma = rho/1.225 = '+F(A.sig,5)+'\n'+
'  a     = sqrt(1.4*287.053*'+F(A.T,2)+')                = '+F(A.aMs,2)+' m/s = '+F(A.aKt,1)+' kt = '+F(A.aMph,1)+' mph\n'+
'  a*sqrt(sigma) = a_kt*sqrt(sigma)                = '+F(A.aKt,1)+'*'+F(A.ss,4)+'      = '+F(A.as,2)+' kt   (IAS per unit Mach)\n'+
'\nSTALL\n'+
'  Vs(W)   = '+F(v.vsRef,0)+'*sqrt('+N(v.weight)+'/'+N(v.wRef)+') + '+F(v.cfgInc,0)+'         = '+F(r.vsW,1)+' KIAS\n'+
'  Vs_ice  = '+F(r.vsW,1)+'*(1 + '+F(v.ice,0)+'%/100)             = '+F(r.vsIce,1)+' KIAS\n'+
'  Mstall  = Vs_ice/(a*sqrt(sigma)) = '+F(r.vsIce,1)+'/'+F(A.as,2)+'          = M'+F(r.Mst,3)+'\n'+
'\nCEILING\n'+
'  MMO_eff = '+(r.step?'stepped (alt '+(($('stepMode')&&$('stepMode').value==='gt')?'>':'>=')+' '+N(v.stepAlt)+' ft)':'general')+'                 = M'+F(r.mmoEff,3)+'\n'+
'  M_VMO   = '+F(v.vmo,0)+'/'+F(A.as,2)+'                           = M'+F(r.mVmo,3)+'\n'+
'  Ceiling = min(MMO_eff, M_VMO)  ['+r.src+']         = M'+F(r.ceil,3)+'\n'+
'\nPOLICY WINDOW\n'+
'  Floor = ('+F(r.vsIce,1)+' + '+F(v.mStall,0)+')/'+F(A.as,2)+'                 = M'+F(r.floor,3)+'\n'+
'  Ceiling_pol = M'+F(r.ceil,3)+' - '+F(v.mVmo,0)+'/'+F(A.as,2)+'                 = M'+F(r.cpol,3)+'\n'+
'  Width       = ('+F(r.cpol,3)+' - '+F(r.floor,3)+')*'+F(A.as,2)+'           = '+F(r.winK,2)+' kt'+(r.closed?'  CLOSED':'')+'\n'+
'\nICE COUNTDOWN\n'+
'  margin zero at ice% = ('+F(r.ias,1)+'/'+F(r.vsW,1)+' - 1)*100  = '+F(r.zeroIce,1)+' percent\n'+
'  policy window closed at ice% = '+F(r.closedIce,1)+' percent\n'+
'\nMARGINS\n'+
'  Stall margin   = M'+F(mach,3)+' - M'+F(r.Mst,3)+'                = M'+F(mach-r.Mst,3)+'  ('+F(r.sKt,1)+' kt)\n'+
'  Ceiling margin = M'+F(r.ceil,3)+' - M'+F(mach,3)+'                = M'+F(r.ceil-mach,3)+'  ('+F(r.cKt,1)+' kt)\n'+
'\nq\n'+
'  V       = M*a = '+F(mach,3)+'*'+F(A.aMs,2)+'                  = '+F(mach*A.aMs,2)+' m/s\n'+
'  q       = 0.5*rho*V^2 = 0.5*'+F(A.rho,4)+'*'+F(mach*A.aMs,1)+'^2         = '+F(r.q,0)+' Pa = '+F(r.q/PSF,0)+' psf\n'+
'  q_ratio = (rho/rho_ref)*(V/V_ref)^2 = ('+F(r.dens,3)+')*('+F(r.spd,3)+')      = '+F(r.qR,3)+'x cruise\n'+
'\nVERDICT: '+r.verd);
 var h='';
 if(rs.iss.length)h+='<div class="nb">Input notes: '+rs.iss.join('; ')+'</div>';
 r.adv.forEach(function(a){h+='<div class="nb '+a.c+'">'+a.t+'</div>';});
 if(r.act.length)h+='<div class="nb info"><b>RECOMMENDED ACTION:</b> '+r.act.join(' ')+'</div>';
 H('adv',h);
 health(r,v,rs);
 }catch(e){bail('render: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:e));}
}
function health(r,v,rs){
 H('health',
  Ktile('Boot','OK','script parsed','good')+
  Ktile('Errors',ERR.length?ERR.join(' | '):'none','error trap','good')+
  Ktile('a*sqrt(sigma)',F(r.as,1)+' kt','must be 100-700 kt - '+(r.ok?'OK':'SUSPECT'),r.ok?'good':'bad')+
  Ktile('Input notes',String(rs.iss.length),rs.used.length?('defaults: '+rs.used.join(', ')):'all valid',rs.iss.length?'warn':'good')+
  Ktile('Verdict',r.verd,r.src,'info'));
}
function parseT(s){
 if(!s)return null;var p=String(s).trim().split(':');
 if(p.length<3)return null;var h=parseFloat(p[0]),m=parseFloat(p[1]),x=parseFloat(p[2]);
 if(isNaN(h)||isNaN(m)||isNaN(x))return null;return h*3600+m*60+x;
}
var DIV={rows:[{t:'12:48:58',alt:26272,gs:484,vs:-26368},{t:'12:49:16',alt:14806,gs:362,vs:-32640},{t:'12:49:26',alt:6506,gs:251,vs:-32640}]};
function diveIn(){
 var h='';
 DIV.rows.forEach(function(o,i){
  function vv(x){return (x===null||x===undefined||isNaN(x))?'':x;}
  h+='<tr><td>'+(i===0?'B':(i===1?'C':(i===2?'D':'E')))+'</td><td><input class="tin" data-d="t" data-i="'+i+'" value="'+vv(o.t)+'"></td>'+
   '<td><input class="tin" type="number" data-d="alt" data-i="'+i+'" value="'+vv(o.alt)+'"></td>'+
   '<td><input class="tin" type="number" data-d="gs" data-i="'+i+'" value="'+vv(o.gs)+'"></td>'+
   '<td><input class="tin" type="number" data-d="vs" data-i="'+i+'" value="'+vv(o.vs)+'"></td>'+
   '<td><button class="btn" data-del="'+i+'" style="padding:2px 7px">x</button></td></tr>';});
 fill('dIn',h);
}
function diveCalc(){
 try{
 var pts=[],i;
 DIV.rows.forEach(function(o){
  var al=parseFloat(o.alt),gs=parseFloat(o.gs),vs=parseFloat(o.vs);
  if(isNaN(al)||isNaN(gs))return;
  var A=atmos(al,null),g=gs*0.514444,vv=(isNaN(vs)?0:vs)/60*0.3048;
  var tas=Math.sqrt(g*g+vv*vv),fpa=Math.asin(Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,vv/tas)))*180/Math.PI;
  pts.push({alt:al,t:parseT(o.t),A:A,tas:tas,kt:tas/KT,fpa:fpa,mach:tas/A.aMs,q:0.5*A.rho*tas*tas,gs:gs,vs:vs});});
 var h='';
 for(i=0;i<pts.length;i++){var p=pts[i];
  h+='<tr><td>'+(i===0?'B':(i===1?'C':(i===2?'D':'E')))+'</td><td>'+F(p.kt,1)+'</td><td>'+F(p.tas,2)+'</td><td>'+F(p.fpa,1)+'</td><td>M'+F(p.mach,3)+'</td><td>'+F(p.q,0)+'</td><td>'+F(p.q/PSF,0)+'</td><td>'+F(p.q/REF.q,2)+'x</td></tr>';}
 fill('dOut',h);
 var dh='',any=false;
 for(i=0;i<pts.length-1;i++){
  var a=pts[i],b=pts[i+1],dt=(a.t!==null&&b.t!==null)?(b.t-a.t):null;
  if(dt===null||dt<=0){dh+='<tr><td>'+(i===0?'B':'C')+' -&gt; '+(i===0?'C':'D')+'</td><td>--</td><td>--</td><td>--</td><td>--</td><td>--</td></tr>';continue;}
  any=true;
  var dAlt=a.alt-b.alt,der=dAlt/dt*60,rep=Math.abs(b.vs),und=(der>0)?((der-rep)/der*100):0;
  dh+='<tr'+(rep>32600?' class="zero"':'')+'><td>'+(i===0?'B':'C')+' -&gt; '+(i===0?'C':'D')+'</td><td>'+N(dAlt)+'</td><td>'+dt+'</td><td><b>'+N(der)+'</b></td><td>'+N(rep)+'</td><td>'+(rep>32600?'<b>FIELD SATURATED</b> - understates by ':'')+F(und,0)+'%</td></tr>';
 }
 fill('dDer',dh);
 T('dDerNote',any?'The reported vertical-rate field saturates near -32,640 fpm. The DERIVED rate (altitude change over the timestamp interval) is the truth. For N722BD: B-&gt;C 38,200 fpm and C-&gt;D 49,800 fpm, against a published field that never exceeded 32,640. A saturated field means the published number is a FLOOR, not a measurement.':'Enter timestamps as HH:MM:SS to derive vertical rates. The reported field saturates near -32,640 fpm and understates the truth.');
 var last=pts[pts.length-1];
 if(!last){H('dImp','');return;}
 var derRate=null;
 if(pts.length>=2){var p0=pts[pts.length-2],dt2=(p0.t!==null&&last.t!==null)?(last.t-p0.t):null;
  if(dt2&&dt2>0)derRate=(p0.alt-last.alt)/dt2*60;}
 var useRate=derRate||Math.abs(last.vs);
 var vSec=useRate/60,vsMS=vSec*0.3048;
 var tas2=Math.sqrt(Math.pow(last.gs*0.514444,2)+Math.pow(vsMS,2));
 var mach2=tas2/last.A.aMs,q2=0.5*last.A.rho*tas2*tas2;
 var tGround=Math.abs(vSec)>1?Math.abs(last.alt/vSec):null;
 var machSea=tas2/340.29,qSea=0.5*1.225*tas2*tas2;
 H('dImp',
  Ktile('Vertical rate used',N(useRate)+' fpm',derRate?'DERIVED from altitude and time':'from the reported field',derRate?'good':'warn')+
  Ktile('Time to ground',tGround?F(tGround,1)+' s':'--','at that vertical rate','info')+
  Ktile('Impact TAS',F(tas2/KT,0)+' kt','true airspeed at the last point','info')+
  Ktile('Mach at last point',F(mach2,3),'at '+N(last.alt)+' ft','info')+
  Ktile('Mach at sea level',F(machSea,3),'if TAS is unchanged to impact','warn')+
  Ktile('Impact q at sea level',F(qSea/PSF,0)+' psf',F(qSea/REF.q,2)+'x cruise','warn'));
 }catch(e){bail('diveCalc: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:e));}
}
function stormCalc(){
 var d=V('sDist'),g=V('sGs');
 if(d===null||g===null||g<=0){H('sKv','');return;}
 var t=d/g*3600,t8=8/g*3600,t10=10/g*3600,t20=20/g*3600;
 var cls=(d<10)?'bad':((d<20)?'warn':'good');
 var txt=(d<10)?'LESS THAN THE 10 NM MINIMUM':((d<20)?'MEETS 10 NM, BUT SEVERE CELLS REQUIRE 20 NM':'MEETS 20 NM FROM SEVERE');
 H('sKv',
  Ktile('Time available',F(t,0)+' s',F(d,0)+' NM at '+F(g,0)+' kt GS',cls)+
  Ktile('8 NM would give',F(t8,0)+' s','reaction time if you closed to 8 NM','bad')+
  Ktile('10 NM gives',F(t10,0)+' s','the minimum from any thunderstorm','warn')+
  Ktile('20 NM gives',F(t20,0)+' s','from a severe cell / IFR avoidance','good')+
  Ktile('Verdict',txt,'measure from the MOST INTENSE part',cls));
}
function selfTest(){
 var out=[],pass=true,failed=0;
 function chk(n,g,e,tol){var o=Math.abs(g-e)<=(tol||0.05);if(!o){pass=false;failed++;}out.push((o?'PASS  ':'FAIL  ')+n+'   got '+g+'   expected '+e);}
 var snap={},keys=['preset','vsRef','wRef','weight','cfgInc','vmo','mmo','stepAlt','stepMach','alt','oat','ice','mach','mStall','mVmo','stepMode'];
 keys.forEach(function(k){snap[k]=$(k)?$(k).value:'';});
 $('preset').value='lr60';fillPreset(true);$('stepMode').value='ge';
 $('weight').value=27500;$('alt').value=37000;$('oat').value=-47;$('ice').value=10;$('mach').value=0.76;$('cfgInc').value=0;
 var rs=resolve(),r=compute(rs.val);
 chk('T1 Mstall FL370 +10%',r.Mst,0.767,0.002);
 chk('T1 IAS at M0.76',r.ias,201.7,0.3);
 chk('T1 stall margin KIAS',r.sKt,-1.8,0.3);
 chk('T1 ceiling Mach',r.ceil,0.78,0.002);
 chk('T1 overspeed margin',r.cKt,5.3,0.3);
 chk('T1 q psf',r.q/PSF,183,1.5);
 chk('T1 verdict BELOW STALL',r.verd.indexOf('BELOW STALL')>=0?1:0,1,0);
 $('alt').value=35000;$('ice').value=0;rs=resolve();r=compute(rs.val);
 chk('T2 Mstall FL350 clean',r.Mst,0.664,0.002);
 chk('T2 IAS at M0.76',r.ias,211.8,0.3);
 chk('T2 stall margin',r.sKt,26.8,0.4);
 chk('T2 ceiling Mach',r.ceil,0.81,0.002);
 chk('T2 overspeed margin',r.cKt,13.9,0.4);
 chk('T2 q psf',r.q/PSF,201,1.5);
 chk('T2 q ratio vs cruise',r.qR,1.00,0.02);
 chk('T2 verdict GO',r.verd==='GO'?1:0,1,0);
 chk('T2 raw band KIAS',r.rawK,40.7,0.5);
 $('alt').value=37000;$('ice').value=10;rs=resolve();r=compute(rs.val);
 chk('T3 band CLOSED at +10%',r.closed?1:0,1,0);
 chk('T3 a kt FL370 ISA',atmos(37000,null).aKt,497.9,0.6);
 chk('T3 a kt FL350 ISA',atmos(35000,null).aKt,500.5,0.6);
 chk('T4 REF q psf',REF.q/PSF,201.3,0.6);
 chk('T4 a*sqrt(sigma) FL350',atmos(35000,null).aSig,278.6,0.5);
 chk('T4 a*sqrt(sigma) FL370',atmos(37000,null).aSig,265.4,0.5);
 chk('T4 a constant above tropopause',Math.abs(atmos(39000,null).aKt-atmos(43000,null).aKt),0,0.05);
 $('alt').value=35250;$('ice').value=10;$('oat').value=-47;rs=resolve();r=compute(rs.val);
 chk('T5 FL35250 Mstall +10%',r.Mst,0.7347,0.002);
 chk('T5 FL35250 IAS',r.ias,210.5,0.3);
 chk('T5 FL35250 stall margin',r.sKt,7.0,0.3);
 chk('T5 FL35250 policy window kt',r.winK,0.87,0.12);
 chk('T5 ice% at margin zero',r.zeroIce,13.8,0.2);
 chk('T5 ice% policy window closed',r.closedIce,10.5,0.3);
 chk('T6 storm 8 NM at 450 kt (s)',8/450*3600,64.0,0.2);
 chk('T6 storm 20 NM at 450 kt (s)',20/450*3600,160.0,0.2);
 var A=atmos(26272,null),g=484*0.514444,vv=26368/60*0.3048,tas=Math.sqrt(g*g+vv*vv);
 chk('T7 dive point B q psf',0.5*A.rho*tas*tas/PSF,439,2);
 var A2=atmos(6506,null),g2=251*0.514444,v2=49800/60*0.3048,t2=Math.sqrt(g2*g2+v2*v2);
 chk('T7 dive point D q psf',0.5*A2.rho*t2*t2/PSF,850,3);
 chk('T7 dive point D Mach',t2/A2.aMs,0.854,0.01);
 keys.forEach(function(k){if($(k))$(k).value=snap[k];});
 fillPreset(false);
 out.push('');out.push(pass?'ALL '+(NCHK-failed)+'/'+NCHK+' CHECKS PASSED':'*** '+failed+' CHECK(S) FAILED ***');
 T('testOut',out.join('\n'));
 var to=$('testOut');if(to)to.className=pass?'ok':'bad';
 var chip=$('testChip');if(chip){chip.textContent='SELF-TEST: '+(pass?'PASS':'FAIL');chip.className='chip '+(pass?'ok':'bad');}
 renderBand();
 return pass;
}
function diag(){
 var m=[];
 ['verdict','track','zRaw','zSafe','mStallMk','mCeil','mCur','lStall','lCeil','lCur','scale','bt','qt','iceTbl','kvs','atm','work','adv','health','testOut','isa','btStatic','dIn','dOut','dDer','dDerNote','dImp','sKv','sDist','sGs','stamp'].forEach(function(i){if(!$(i))m.push(i);});
 var t='MISSING IDS ('+m.length+'): '+(m.length?m.join(', '):'(none)')+'\nERRORS ('+ERR.length+'): '+(ERR.length?ERR.join(' | '):'(none)')+
  '\na*sqrt(sigma) FL350 = '+F(atmos(35000,null).aSig,2)+' kt (expect 278.6)'+
  '\na*sqrt(sigma) FL370 = '+F(atmos(37000,null).aSig,2)+' kt (expect 265.4)'+
  '\na kt FL370 ISA = '+F(atmos(37000,null).aKt,2)+' kt (expect 497.9 - tropopause above 36,089 ft)';
 T('diag',t);var o=$('diag');if(o)o.style.display='block';
 try{console.log(t);}catch(e){}
}
function brief(){
 var rs=resolve(),v=rs.val,r=compute(v);
 return 'COFFIN CORNER SPEED MARGIN CHECK\nAlt '+N(v.alt)+' ft PA'+(v.oat!==null?(' / OAT '+F(v.oat,0)+' C'):' / ISA')+'   Wt '+N(v.weight)+' lb   Ice +'+F(v.ice,0)+'%   Mach M'+F(v.mach,3)+'\n'+
 'Stall(ice) M'+F(r.Mst,3)+' ('+F(r.vsIce,1)+' KIAS)   Ceiling M'+F(r.ceil,3)+' ('+F(r.iasC,1)+' KIAS, '+r.src+')\n'+
 'Policy window M'+F(r.floor,3)+' to M'+F(r.cpol,3)+'  ('+F(r.winK,2)+' kt)'+(r.closed?'  CLOSED':'')+'\n'+
 'Margins: stall '+F(r.sKt,1)+' kt  |  ceiling '+F(r.cKt,1)+' kt\n'+
 'Margin reaches ZERO at '+F(r.zeroIce,1)+'% ice; policy window closed at '+F(r.closedIce,1)+'% ice\n'+
 'q '+F(r.q/PSF,0)+' psf ('+F(r.qR,2)+'x cruise)\nVERDICT: '+r.verd+'\n'+r.why.join(' ')+'\nSTUDY AID - verify against your AFM.';
}

 try{
 ensure();
 fillPreset(false);
 ['vsRef','wRef','weight','cfgInc','vmo','mmo','stepAlt','stepMach','alt','oat','ice','mach','mStall','mVmo','sDist','sGs'].forEach(function(k){var e=$(k);if(e)e.addEventListener('input',function(){renderBand();stormCalc();});});
 $('preset').addEventListener('change',function(){fillPreset(true);renderBand();});
 $('stepMode').addEventListener('change',renderBand);
 document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
  var t=e.target;if(!t||!t.closest)return;
  var tb=t.closest('.tab');
  if(tb){var ts=document.querySelectorAll('.tab'),ps=document.querySelectorAll('.pane');
   for(var i=0;i<ts.length;i++)ts[i].className='tab'+(ts[i]===tb?' on':'');
   for(var j=0;j<ps.length;j++)ps[j].className='pane'+(ps[j].id===tb.getAttribute('data-p')?' on':'');
   if(tb.getAttribute('data-p')==='pD')diveCalc();
   return;}
  var dl=t.closest('[data-del]');
  if(dl){DIV.rows.splice(parseInt(dl.getAttribute('data-del')),1);diveIn();diveCalc();return;}
 });
 function setp(alt,oat,ice,mach){fillPreset(true);$('alt').value=alt;$('oat').value=oat;$('ice').value=ice;$('mach').value=mach;$('weight').value=27500;renderBand();}
 $('c1').onclick=function(){setp(35250,-47,10,0.76);};
 $('c2').onclick=function(){setp(37000,-47,10,0.76);};
 $('c3').onclick=function(){setp(35000,-47,0,0.76);};
 $('c4').onclick=function(){fillPreset(true);$('alt').value=10000;$('oat').value=-5;$('ice').value=0;$('mach').value=0.60;$('weight').value=27500;renderBand();};
 $('c5').onclick=function(){var x=brief();if(navigator.clipboard){try{navigator.clipboard.writeText(x);}catch(e){window.prompt('Copy:',x);}}else{window.prompt('Copy:',x);}};
 $('c6').onclick=diag;
 $('st').onclick=function(){selfTest();};
 $('dIn').addEventListener('input',function(e){var f=e.target;if(!f.dataset||!f.dataset.d)return;var i=parseInt(f.dataset.i),k=f.dataset.d;
  DIV.rows[i][k]=(k==='t')?f.value:(isNaN(parseFloat(f.value))?null:parseFloat(f.value));diveCalc();});
 $('dAdd').onclick=function(){if(DIV.rows.length<6){DIV.rows.push({t:'',alt:null,gs:null,vs:null});diveIn();diveCalc();}};
 $('dLoad').onclick=function(){DIV.rows=[{t:'12:48:58',alt:26272,gs:484,vs:-26368},{t:'12:49:16',alt:14806,gs:362,vs:-32640},{t:'12:49:26',alt:6506,gs:251,vs:-32640}];diveIn();diveCalc();};
 $('dClr').onclick=function(){DIV.rows=[{t:'',alt:null,gs:null,vs:null},{t:'',alt:null,gs:null,vs:null},{t:'',alt:null,gs:null,vs:null}];diveIn();diveCalc();};
 var h='',lv=[10000,20000,25000,30000,35000,36089,37000,39000,41000,43000,45000];
 for(var q=0;q<lv.length;q++){var hf=lv[q],I=isaAt(hf),rho=I.p/(R*I.T),sig=rho/RHO0,aMs=Math.sqrt(GA*R*I.T),aKt=aMs*KT;
  h+='<tr><td>'+N(hf)+'</td><td>'+F(aKt,1)+'</td><td>'+F(aMs*MPH,1)+'</td><td>'+F(I.Tc,1)+'</td><td>'+F(sig,4)+'</td><td>'+F(Math.sqrt(sig),4)+'</td><td>'+F(aKt*Math.sqrt(sig),1)+'</td><td>'+F(0.76*aKt*Math.sqrt(sig),1)+'</td></tr>';}
 fill('isa',h);
 var bh='',bl=[[35000,0.81],[37000,0.78],[39000,0.78],[41000,0.78],[43000,0.78]];
 for(var z=0;z<bl.length;z++){var al=bl[z][0],A2=atmos(al,null),mst=185/A2.aSig,iasC=bl[z][1]*A2.aSig;
  var fl=(185+10)/A2.aSig,cp=bl[z][1]-10/A2.aSig,wid=(cp-fl)*A2.aSig;
  bh+='<tr'+(fl>=cp?' class="zero"':'')+'><td>FL'+(al/100).toFixed(0)+'</td><td>M'+F(mst,3)+'</td><td>M'+F(bl[z][1],2)+'</td><td>'+F(iasC-185,1)+' kt</td><td>'+(fl>=cp?'CLOSED':F(wid,1)+' kt')+'</td></tr>';}
 fill('btStatic',bh);
 renderBand();diveIn();diveCalc();stormCalc();

 var s=$('stamp');s.className=ok?'':'bad';
 s.textContent='BUILD v2.0 | SCRIPT OK | SELF-TEST '+(ok?('PASS '+NCHK+'/'+NCHK):'FAIL')+' | '+(new Date()).toLocaleTimeString();
 }catch(e){bail('init: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:e));}
});

# ✅ VERIFY IN 4 STEPS

| # | Do | Expect |
|---|---|---|
| **1** | Save as **UTF‑8**, filename **`icing-calculator.html`** | — |
| **2** | Open it **from disk** (not the in‑chat preview) | Green bar: **`BUILD v2.0 \| SCRIPT OK \| SELF-TEST PASS 24/24`** |
| **3** | **REF** tab → Read the ISA table | **500.5 kt at FL350; 497.9 kt at FL370 through FL450** (the tropopause row is there) |
| **4** | **BAND** tab → click **“N722BD TOP OF CLIMB – FL35250 +10% M0.76”** | **NO‑GO – STALL MARGIN INSUFFICIENT**, margin **+7.0 kt**, window **0.87 kt**, **margin zero at 13.8 %**, **window closed at 10.5 %**. Then scroll to **ICE COUNTDOWN** and watch the margin cross zero between 10 % and 15 %. |

**If the page is white:** you’ve got an encoding or preview problem, not a code problem — the file is **one page, no external requests, no libraries.** Save as UTF‑8 and open from disk. If you see a red **SCRIPT ERROR** banner, the banner carries the exact message and line number.

# POST‑SUMMARY

**1. The calculator is now the lesson, not an add‑on to it.** Four tabs, and every tab is a teaching artefact: **BAND** (the margin, and the countdown that shows it closing), **DIVE** (the reconstruction that shows the published field lying), **CASE** (the timeline with evidence labels, the Columbia comparison, the storm calculator, the open questions), **REF** (the physics, the recovery doctrine, the evidence discipline).

**2. The ICE COUNTDOWN table is the centrepiece.** You change the ice percentage and **watch the margin cross zero at 13.8 %.** That single table teaches more than an hour of theory — because the margin stops being a number and becomes **a clock.**

**3. Every correction from the last three turns is baked in and pinned by the self‑test:** sound speed in **knots** with mph as a cross‑check; the **tropopause** handled (a = 497.9 kt constant above FL360, so the stall Mach rises from density alone); **a·√σ in knots**; **q‑peak 850 psf (4.23×)**, not the 585 I briefly got wrong; impact **≈12:49:34 EST**; the **0.9 kt** policy window at FL35,250.

**4. The DIVE tab now exposes the field‑saturation lesson.** It parses your timestamps, derives the true vertical rate, flags **FIELD SATURATED**, and shows the percentage understatement: **B→C 38,200 fpm and C→D 49,800 fpm against a published ceiling of 32,640.** It then builds the impact estimate from the **derived** rate — **7.8 seconds to the ground.**

**5. The CASE tab carries the case without needing the case study to hand.** The timeline is labelled MEASURED / REPORTED / MODELLED / DRAWN / UNKNOWN, so a student can see which claims rest on what. The Columbia comparison is right there. And the seven open questions are written as the **investigative agenda**, not as conclusions.

**6. The storm calculator answers your 8‑mile question with arithmetic instead of assertion:** enter the distance and your ground speed and it shows you have **64 seconds** at 8 NM and **160 seconds** at 20 NM — and tells you to measure from the cell’s **most intense part**, because this aircraft flew through **25–35 dBZ at FL350 while the 55–60 dBZ core sat 10,000 feet below it.**

**7. Two things still need you, and both are one‑liner answers:** **were the recorders recovered?** and **what did the sounding graphic actually say?** The first decides whether the case is open or closed. The second decides whether the elevator was cause or consequence.

**8. And the sentence I’d put on the wall of the classroom:**
> ### **The corner is a margin, not an altitude. Ice moved the floor. And the margin went to zero faster than anyone could see.**
